'./components/SolarSystemCanvas';\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [selectedPlanetId, setSelectedPlanetId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [settings, setSettings] = ...prev,\n      speedMultiplier: parseFloat(e.target.value),\n    }));\n  };\n\n  const toggleOrbits = () => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, showOrbits: !prev.showOrbits }));\n  };\n\n  const data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/SolarSystemCanvas.tsx","content":"import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';\nimport * as THREE from 'three';\nimport { CelestialBody, settings?: SimulationSettings;\n}\n\ninterface PlanetMeshObject {\n  id: string;\n  body: CelestialBody;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  group: THREE.Group; // 公転用グループ（原点中心）\n  orbitLine: THREE.Line;\n  labelSprite?: THREE.Sprite;\n  labelTexture?: THREE.CanvasTexture;\n  currentAngle: number;\n}\n\nconst SolarSystemCanvas = ({\n  planets = [],\n  selectedPlanetId = null,\n  onSelectPlanet,\n  settings = {\n    isPlaying: true,\n    speedMultiplier: 1,\n    showOrbits: true,\n    showLabels: true,\n  },\n}: SolarSystemCanvasProps) => {\n  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n\n  // Three.js 内部オブジェクトの参照保持\n  const refs = useRef<{\n    scene: THREE.Scene;\n    camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;\n    renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer;\n    planetObjects: PlanetMeshObject[];\n    isInteracting: boolean;\n    prevPointer: { x: number; y: number };\n    spherical: THREE.Spherical;\n    targetFocus: THREE.Vector3;\n    currentFocus: THREE.Vector3;\n    cameraDistance: number;\n    targetCameraDistance: number;\n    reqId: number | null;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  // settingsの変更をThreeのレンダリングループから安全に参照するためのRef\n  const settingsRef = useRef(settings);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n    // 軌道線・ラベルの即時可視性反映\n    if (refs.current) {\n      refs.current.planetObjects.forEach((po) => {\n        po.orbitLine.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n        if (po.labelSprite) {\n          po.labelSprite.visible = settings.showLabels;\n        }\n      });\n    }\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  // selectedPlanetIdの変更を安全に参照するためのRef\n  const selectedPlanetIdRef = useRef(selectedPlanetId);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    selectedPlanetIdRef.current = selectedPlanetId;\n    if (refs.current) {\n      if (!selectedPlanetId) {\n        refs.current.targetFocus.set(0, 0, 0);\n        refs.current.targetCameraDistance = 110;\n      } else {\n        const found = refs.current.planetObjects.find((p) => p.id === selectedPlanetId);\n        if (found) {\n          const bodyRadius = found.body.radius;\n          refs.current.targetCameraDistance = Math.max(bodyRadius * 4.5, 12);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }, [selectedPlanetId]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const container = containerRef.current;\n    if (!container) return;\n\n    const width = container.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;\n    const height = container.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;\n\n    // 1. シーン構築\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x030712, 0.0012);\n\n    // 2. カメラ構築\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, width / height, 0.1, 2000);\n    const initialSpherical = new THREE.Spherical(110, Math.PI / 3, Math.PI / 4);\n    camera.position.setFromSpherical(initialSpherical);\n\n    // 3. レンダラー構築\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // 4. 照明設定\n    const ambientLight = new THREE.AmbientLight(0xffffff, 0.25);\n    scene.add(ambientLight);\n\n    const sunPointLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff, 2.2, 800, 0.4);\n    sunPointLight.position.set(0, 0, 0);\n    scene.add(sunPointLight);\n\n    // 5. 背景の星空パーティクル\n    const starCount = 3500;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);\n    const starColors = new Float32Array(starCount * 3);\n\n    const colorA = new THREE.Color(0xffffff);\n    const colorB = new THREE.Color(0x93c5fd);\n    const colorC = new THREE.Color(0xfef08a);\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {\n      const i3 = i * 3;\n      const radius = 500 + Math.random() * 800;\n      const theta = 2 * Math.PI * Math.random();\n      const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);\n\n      starPositions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const mixed = Math.random();\n      const choice = mixed < 0.7 ? colorA : mixed < 0.9 ? colorB : colorC;\n      starColors[i3] = choice.r;\n      starColors[i3 + 1] = choice.g;\n      starColors[i3 + 2] = choice.b;\n    }\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.8,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85,\n    });\n    const starPoints = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);\n    scene.add(starPoints);\n\n    // テキストスプライト生成ヘルパー\n    const createLabelSprite = (text: string): { sprite: THREE.Sprite; texture: THREE.CanvasTexture } => {\n      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      canvas.width = 256;\n      canvas.height = 64;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (ctx) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.8)';\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        if (typeof ctx.roundRect === 'function') {\n          ctx.roundRect(10, 10, 236, 44, 20);\n        } else {\n          ctx.rect(10, 10, 236, 44);\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.5)';\n        ctx.stroke();\n\n        ctx.font = 'bold 22px -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif';\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n        ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n        ctx.fillText(text, 128, 32);\n      }\n      const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);\n      texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;\n      const spriteMaterial = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: texture, transparent: true, depthTest: false });\n      const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(spriteMaterial);\n      sprite.scale.set(7, 1.75, 1);\n      return { sprite, texture };\n    };\n\n    // 6. 天体メッシュ群の構築\n    const planetObjects: PlanetMeshObject[] = [];\n\n    planets.forEach((body) => {\n      const orbitGroup = new THREE.Group();\n      scene.add(orbitGroup);\n\n      // 球体メッシュ作成\n      const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius, 32, 32);\n      let material: THREE.Material;\n\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color),\n        });\n      } else {\n        material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color),\n          roughness: 0.65,\n          metalness: 0.15,\n        });\n      }\n\n      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, material);\n      mesh.castShadow = true;\n      mesh.receiveShadow = true;\n      mesh.position.x = body.distance;\n      orbitGroup.add(mesh);\n\n      // 太陽コロナグロー\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        const glowGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius * 1.25, 32, 32);\n        const glowMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: 0xffaa33,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.25,\n          side: THREE.BackSide,\n        });\n        const glowMesh = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeo, glowMat);\n        mesh.add(glowMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 土星の輪\n      if (body.hasRings && body.ringInnerRadius && body.ringOuterRadius) {\n        const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(body.ringInnerRadius, body.ringOuterRadius, 64);\n        const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.ringColor || '#e2d4a8'),\n          side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.85,\n          roughness: 0.5,\n        });\n        const ringMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, ringMat);\n        ringMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI * 0.45;\n        ringMesh.rotation.y = Math.PI * 0.1;\n        mesh.add(ringMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 軌道ライン (LineLoop)\n      const orbitCurve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(0, 0, body.distance, body.distance, 0, 2 * Math.PI, false, 0);\n      const orbitPoints = orbitCurve.getPoints(120);\n      const orbitGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(\n        orbitPoints.map((p) => new THREE.Vector3(p.x, 0, p.y))\n      );\n      const orbitMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n        color: 0x38bdf8,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: body.distance === 0 ? 0 : 0.28,\n      });\n      const orbitLine = new THREE.Line(orbitGeo, orbitMat);\n      scene.add(orbitLine);\n      orbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n\n      // ラベルスプライト\n      let labelSprite: THREE.Sprite | undefined;\n      let labelTexture: THREE.CanvasTexture | undefined;\n      if (body.id !== 'sun') {\n        const labelObj = createLabelSprite(body.name);\n        labelSprite = labelObj.sprite;\n        labelTexture = labelObj.texture;\n        labelSprite.position.set(0, body.radius + 2.5, 0);\n        mesh.add(labelSprite);\n        labelSprite.visible = settingsRef.current.showLabels;\n      }\n\n      planetObjects.push({\n        id: body.id,\n        body,\n        mesh,\n        group: orbitGroup,\n        orbitLine,\n        labelSprite,\n        labelTexture,\n        currentAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n      });\n    });\n\n    // 内部ステート初期化\n    refs.current = {\n      scene,\n      camera,\n      renderer,\n      planetObjects,\n      isInteracting: false,\n      prevPointer: { x: 0, y: 0 },\n      spherical: initialSpherical,\n      targetFocus: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0),\n      currentFocus: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0),\n      cameraDistance: 110,\n      targetCameraDistance: 110,\n      reqId: null,\n    };\n\n    // 7. マウス・タッチ操作（OrbitControls相当の軽量実装）\n    const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (e.button !== 0 && e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      refs.current.isInteracting = true;\n      refs.current.prevPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!refs.current || !refs.current.isInteracting) return;\n      const dx = e.clientX - refs.current.prevPointer.x;\n      const dy = e.clientY - refs.current.prevPointer.y;\n      refs.current.prevPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n\n      const rotateSpeed = 0.005;\n      refs.current.spherical.theta -= dx * rotateSpeed;\n      refs.current.spherical.phi -= dy * rotateSpeed;\n\n      // 仰角制限 (真上・真下でのフリップ防止)\n      refs.current.spherical.phi = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.08, refs.current.spherical.phi));\n    };\n\n    const onPointerUp = () => {\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      refs.current.isInteracting = false;\n    };\n\n    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      const zoomFactor = 1 + e.deltaY * 0.0012;\n      refs.current.targetCameraDistance = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(\n        refs.current.targetCameraDistance * zoomFactor,\n        6,\n        350\n      );\n    };\n\n    // レイキャストクリック判定\n    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n    const mouseVector = new THREE.Vector2();\n\n    const onClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (!refs.current || !container) return;\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      mouseVector.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n      mouseVector.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n      raycaster.setFromCamera(mouseVector, refs.current.camera);\n      const meshesToTest = refs.current.planetObjects.map((po) => po.mesh);\n      const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(meshesToTest, false);\n\n      if (intersects.length > 0) {\n        const hitMesh = intersects[0].object as THREE.Mesh;\n        const matched = refs.current.planetObjects.find((po) => po.mesh === hitMesh);\n        if (matched) {\n          onSelectPlanet?.(matched.id);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const domElement = renderer.domElement;\n    domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);\n    domElement.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });\n    domElement.addEventListener('click', onClick);\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !refs.current) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const h = container.clientHeight;\n      refs.current.camera.aspect = w / h;\n      refs.current.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      refs.current.renderer.setSize(w, h);\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // 8. メインレンダリングループ\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    const animate = (time: number) => {\n      const delta = Math.min((time - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = time;\n\n      const state = refs.current;\n      if (!state) return;\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const currentSelectedId = selectedPlanetIdRef.current;\n\n      // 惑星の公転・自転\n      state.planetObjects.forEach((po) => {\n        if (currentSettings.isPlaying) {\n          const moveDelta = po.body.speed * currentSettings.speedMultiplier * delta * 2.5;\n          po.currentAngle += moveDelta;\n          po.mesh.position.x = Math.cos(po.currentAngle) * po.body.distance;\n          po.mesh.position.z = Math.sin(po.currentAngle) * po.body.distance;\n          po.mesh.rotation.y += po.body.rotationSpeed * delta * 60;\n        }\n\n        // 選択惑星の場合はフォーカスターゲットを惑星のワールド座標へ追従\n        if (currentSelectedId === po.id) {\n          po.mesh.getWorldPosition(state.targetFocus);\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 惑星追従時: ユーザーのドラッグ回転と目標位置追従が競合して揺れないよう、\n      // 選択中は直接追従、未選択時はスムーズ補間(Lerp)で原点へ復帰\n      if (currentSelectedId) {\n        state.currentFocus.copy(state.targetFocus);\n      } else {\n        state.currentFocus.lerp(state.targetFocus, 0.06);\n      }\n\n      state.cameraDistance = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(state.cameraDistance, state.targetCameraDistance, 0.08);\n      state.spherical.radius = state.cameraDistance;\n\n      // カメラ位置決定 (独立した球面座標オフセットをターゲット位置に加算)\n      const camOffset = new THREE.Vector3().setFromSpherical(state.spherical);\n      state.camera.position.copy(state.currentFocus).add(camOffset);\n      state.camera.lookAt(state.currentFocus);\n\n      state.renderer.render(state.scene, state.camera);\n      state.reqId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n    };\n\n    refs.current.reqId = requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      if (refs.current?.reqId) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(refs.current.reqId);\n      }\n      domElement.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);\n      domElement.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);\n      domElement.removeEventListener('click', onClick);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n      // 惑星関連リソースの完全な破棄（球体・リング・軌道線・スプライト・テクスチャ）\n      planetObjects.forEach((po) => {\n        // メッシュおよび子要素の走査破棄\n        po.mesh.traverse((child) => {\n          if (child instanceof THREE.Mesh || child instanceof THREE.Sprite) {\n            if (child.geometry) {\n              child.geometry.dispose();\n            }\n            if (child.material) {\n              if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {\n                child.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n              } else {\n                child.material.dispose();\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        });\n\n        // 軌道ラインの破棄\n        if (po.orbitLine) {\n          if (po.orbitLine.geometry) {\n            po.orbitLine.geometry.dispose();\n          }\n          if (po.orbitLine.material) {\n            if (Array.isArray(po.orbitLine.material)) {\n              po.orbitLine.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n            } else {\n              po.orbitLine.material.dispose();\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ラベル用 CanvasTexture の破棄\n        if (po.labelTexture) {\n          po.labelTexture.dispose();\n        }\n      });\n\n      starGeometry.dispose();\n      starMaterial.dispose();\n      renderer.dispose();\n      if (container.contains(domElement)) {\n        container.removeChild(domElement);\n      }\n    };\n  }, [planets, onSelectPlanet]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\" ref={containerRef}>\n      <div className=\"legend-hint\">\n        <span>ドラッグで視点回転</span>\n        <span>ホイールでズームイン・アウト</span>\n        <span>惑星クリックで接近フォーカス</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SolarSystemCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

setSelectedPlanetId(null);\n      }\n    };\n\n    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    return () => {\n      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  // 選択された天体チップを横スクロール領域の中央へ自動スクロール\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (selectedPlanetId && chipRefs.current[selectedPlanetId]) {\n      chipRefs.current[selectedPlanetId]?.scrollIntoView({\n        behavior: 'smooth',\n        inline: 'center',\n        block: 'nearest',\n      });\n    }\n  }, [selectedPlanetId]);\n\n  const togglePlayback = () => {\n    setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, showLabels: !prev.showLabels }));\n  };\n\n  const handleResetCamera = () => {\n    setSelectedPlanetId(null);\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div starColors[i3] = choice.r;\n      starColors[i3 + 1] = choice.g;\n      starColors[i3 + 2] = choice.b;\n    }\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n        ctx.fillText(text, 128, 32);\n      }\n      const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);\n      texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;\n      const scene.add(orbitGroup);\n\n      // 球体メッシュ作成\n      const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius, 32, 32);\n      let material: THREE.Material;\n\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n mesh.receiveShadow = true;\n      mesh.position.x = body.distance;\n      orbitGroup.add(mesh);\n\n      // 太陽コロナグロー\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        const glowGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius * 1.25, 32, 32);\n        const glowMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: 0xffaa33,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.25,\n   const orbitCurve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(0, 0, body.distance, body.distance, 0, 2 * Math.PI, false, 0);\n      const orbitPoints = orbitCurve.getPoints(120);\n      const orbitGeo = new refs.current.prevPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!refs.current || !refs.current.isInteracting) return;\n      const dx };\n\n    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      const zoomFactor = 1 + e.deltaY * 0.0012;\n      refs.current.targetCameraDistance = (intersects.length > 0) {\n        const hitMesh = intersects[0].object as THREE.Mesh;\n        const matched = refs.current.planetObjects.find((po) => po.mesh === hitMesh);\n        if (matched) {\n          onSelectPlanet?.(matched.id);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const domElement = renderer.domElement;\n    domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);\n    domElement.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });\n    domElement.addEventListener('click', onClick);\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !refs.current) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // 8. メインレンダリングループ\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    const animate = (time: number) => {\n      const delta = Math.min((time - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = time;\n\n      const state = refs.current;\n      if (!state) return;\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const currentSelectedId = domElement.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);\n      window.removeEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);\n      domElement.removeEventListener('wheel', onWheel);\n      domElement.removeEventListener('click', onClick);\n      window.removeEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n      // 惑星・衛星関連リソースの完全な破棄（球体・リング・軌道線・衛星・スプライト・テクスチャ）\n      planetObjects.forEach((po) => {\n        // メッシュおよび子要素の走査破棄\n        po.mesh.traverse((child) => {\n          if (child instanceof THREE.Mesh || child instanceof THREE.Sprite) {\n            if (child.geometry) {\n              child.geometry.dispose();\n            }\n            if (child.material) {\n              if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {\n                child.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n              } else {\n                child.material.dispose();\n              }\n            }\n          }\n        });\n\n        // 衛星メッシュ・衛星軌道線の破棄\n        if (po.satelliteGroup) {\n          po.satelliteGroup.traverse((child) => {\n            if (child instanceof THREE.Mesh || child instanceof THREE.Line) {\n              if (child.geometry) {\n                child.geometry.dispose();\n              }\n              if (child.material) {\n                if (Array.isArray(child.material)) {\n                  child.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n                } else {\n                  child.material.dispose();\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          });\n        }\n\n        // 軌道ラインの破棄\n        if (po.orbitLine) {\n          if (po.orbitLine.geometry) {\n            po.orbitLine.geometry.dispose();\n          }\n          if (po.orbitLine.material) {\n            if (Array.isArray(po.orbitLine.material)) {\n              po.orbitLine.material.forEach((m) => m.dispose());\n            } else {\n              po.orbitLine.material.dispose();\n            }\n          }\n        }\n\n        // ラベル用 CanvasTexture の破棄\n        if (po.labelTexture) {\n          po.labelTexture.dispose();\n        }\n };\n  }, [planets, onSelectPlanet]);\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"canvas-container\" ref={containerRef}>\n      <div className=\"legend-hint\">\n        <span>ドラッグで視点回転</span>\n        <span>ホイールでズームイン・アウト</span>\n        <span>惑星クリックで接近フォーカス</span>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default SolarSystemCanvas;","language":"tsx"}

settings?: SimulationSettings;\n}\n\ninterface MoonMeshObject {\n  id: string;\n  data: MoonData;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  orbitLine: THREE.Line;\n  currentAngle: number;\n}\n\ninterface PlanetMeshObject {\n  id: string;\n  body: CelestialBody;\n  mesh: THREE.Mesh;\n  group: THREE.Group; // 公転用グループ（原点中心）\n  satelliteGroup?: THREE.Group; // 衛星用グループ（惑星位置に同期）\n  orbitLine: THREE.Line;\n useRef(settings);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    settingsRef.current = settings;\n    // 軌道線・衛星軌道線・ラベルの即時可視性反映\n    if (refs.current) {\n      refs.current.planetObjects.forEach((po) => {\n        po.orbitLine.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n        if (po.moons) {\n          po.moons.forEach((mo) => {\n            mo.orbitLine.visible = settings.showOrbits;\n          });\n        }\n        if (po.labelSprite) {\n          po.labelSprite.visible = settings.showLabels;\n        }\n      });\n    }\n  }, [settings]);\n\n  // selectedPlanetIdの変更を安全に参照するためのRef\n  const selectedPlanetIdRef = 0);\n        refs.current.targetCameraDistance = 110;\n      } else {\n        const found = refs.current.planetObjects.find((p) => p.id === selectedPlanetId);\n        if (found) {\n          const // 1. シーン構築\n    const scene = new THREE.Scene();\n    scene.fog = new THREE.FogExp2(0x030712, 0.0012);\n\n    // 2. カメラ構築\n    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, width / height, 0.1, 2000);\n    const initialSpherical = new THREE.Spherical(110, Math.PI / 3, Math.PI / 4);\n    camera.position.setFromSpherical(initialSpherical);\n\n    // 3. レンダラー構築\n    const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'high-performance' });\n    renderer.setSize(width, height);\n    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));\n    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;\n    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.2;\n    container.innerHTML = '';\n    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);\n\n    // 4. 照明設定\n sunPointLight.position.set(0, 0, 0);\n    scene.add(sunPointLight);\n\n    // 5. 背景の星空パーティクル\n    const starCount = 3500;\n    const starGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry();\n    const starPositions colorC = new THREE.Color(0xfef08a);\n\n    for (let i = 0; i < starCount; i++) {\n      const i3 = i * 3;\n      const radius = 500 + Math.random() * 800;\n      const theta = 2 * Math.PI * Math.random();\n      const phi = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);\n\n      starPositions[i3] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.cos(theta);\n      starPositions[i3 + 1] = radius * Math.sin(phi) * Math.sin(theta);\n      starPositions[i3 + 2] = radius * Math.cos(phi);\n\n      const mixed = Math.random();\n      const choice = mixed < 0.7 ? colorA : mixed < 0.9 ? colorB : colorC;\n      starColors[i3] = choice.r;\n      starColors[i3 + 1] = choice.g;\n      starColors[i3 + 2] = choice.b;\n    }\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starPositions, 3));\n    starGeometry.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(starColors, 3));\n\n    const starMaterial = new THREE.PointsMaterial({\n      size: 1.8,\n      vertexColors: true,\n      transparent: true,\n      opacity: 0.85,\n    });\n    const starPoints = new THREE.Points(starGeometry, starMaterial);\n    scene.add(starPoints);\n\n    // テキストスプライト生成ヘルパー\n    const createLabelSprite = (text: string): { sprite: THREE.Sprite; texture: THREE.CanvasTexture } => {\n      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n      canvas.width = 256;\n      canvas.height = 64;\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');\n      if (ctx) {\n        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.8)';\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        if (typeof ctx.roundRect === 'function') {\n          ctx.roundRect(10, 10, 236, 44, 20);\n        } else {\n          ctx.rect(10, 10, 236, 44);\n        }\n        ctx.fill();\n        ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.5)';\n        ctx.stroke();\n\n        ctx.font = 'bold 22px -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif';\n        ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';\n        ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n        ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';\n        ctx.fillText(text, 128, 32);\n      }\n      const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);\n      texture.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;\n      const spriteMaterial = new THREE.SpriteMaterial({ map: texture, transparent: true, depthTest: false });\n      const sprite = new THREE.Sprite(spriteMaterial);\n      sprite.scale.set(7, 1.75, 1);\n      return { sprite, texture };\n    };\n\n    // 6. 天体メッシュ群の構築\n    const planetObjects: PlanetMeshObject[] = [];\n\n    planets.forEach((body) => {\n      const orbitGroup = new THREE.Group();\n      scene.add(orbitGroup);\n\n      // 球体メッシュ作成\n      const sphereGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius, 32, 32);\n      let material: THREE.Material;\n\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color),\n        });\n      } else {\n        material = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.color),\n          roughness: 0.65,\n          metalness: 0.15,\n        });\n      }\n\n      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(sphereGeo, material);\n      mesh.castShadow = true;\n      mesh.receiveShadow = true;\n      mesh.position.x = body.distance;\n      orbitGroup.add(mesh);\n\n      // 太陽コロナグロー\n      if (body.id === 'sun') {\n        const glowGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(body.radius * 1.25, 32, 32);\n        const glowMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({\n          color: 0xffaa33,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.25,\n          side: THREE.BackSide,\n        });\n        const glowMesh = new THREE.Mesh(glowGeo, glowMat);\n        mesh.add(glowMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 土星などの輪\n      if (body.hasRings && body.ringInnerRadius && body.ringOuterRadius) {\n        const ringGeo = new THREE.RingGeometry(body.ringInnerRadius, body.ringOuterRadius, 64);\n        const ringMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n          color: new THREE.Color(body.ringColor || '#e2d4a8'),\n          side: THREE.DoubleSide,\n          transparent: true,\n          opacity: 0.85,\n          roughness: 0.5,\n        });\n        const ringMesh = new THREE.Mesh(ringGeo, ringMat);\n        ringMesh.rotation.x = Math.PI * 0.45;\n        ringMesh.rotation.y = Math.PI * 0.1;\n        mesh.add(ringMesh);\n      }\n\n      // 軌道ライン (LineLoop)\n      const orbitCurve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(0, 0, body.distance, body.distance, 0, 2 * Math.PI, false, 0);\n      const orbitPoints = orbitCurve.getPoints(120);\n      const orbitGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(\n        orbitPoints.map((p) => new THREE.Vector3(p.x, 0, p.y))\n      );\n      const orbitMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n        color: 0x38bdf8,\n        transparent: true,\n        opacity: body.distance === 0 ? 0 : 0.28,\n      });\n      const orbitLine = new THREE.Line(orbitGeo, orbitMat);\n      scene.add(orbitLine);\n      orbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n\n      // 衛星（ムーン）の構築\n      let satelliteGroup: THREE.Group | undefined;\n      const moonMeshList: MoonMeshObject[] = [];\n\n      if (body.moons && body.moons.length > 0) {\n        satelliteGroup = new THREE.Group();\n        satelliteGroup.position.copy(mesh.position);\n        scene.add(satelliteGroup);\n\n        body.moons.forEach((m) => {\n          // 衛星の軌道線\n          const moonOrbitCurve = new THREE.EllipseCurve(0, 0, m.distance, m.distance, 0, 2 * Math.PI, false, 0);\n          const moonOrbitPts = moonOrbitCurve.getPoints(64);\n          const moonOrbitGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(\n            moonOrbitPts.map((p) => new THREE.Vector3(p.x, 0, p.y))\n          );\n          const moonOrbitMat = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({\n            color: 0x94a3b8,\n            transparent: true,\n            opacity: 0.22,\n          });\n          const moonOrbitLine = new THREE.Line(moonOrbitGeo, moonOrbitMat);\n          moonOrbitLine.visible = settingsRef.current.showOrbits;\n          satelliteGroup!.add(moonOrbitLine);\n\n          // 衛星球体メッシュ\n          const moonGeo = new THREE.SphereGeometry(m.radius, 16, 16);\n          const moonMat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({\n            color: new THREE.Color(m.color),\n            roughness: 0.75,\n            metalness: 0.1,\n          });\n          const moonMesh = new THREE.Mesh(moonGeo, moonMat);\n          moonMesh.castShadow = true;\n          moonMesh.receiveShadow = true;\n          moonMesh.position.x = m.distance;\n          satelliteGroup!.add(moonMesh);\n\n          moonMeshList.push({\n            id: m.id,\n            data: m,\n            mesh: moonMesh,\n            orbitLine: moonOrbitLine,\n            currentAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n          });\n        });\n      }\n\n      // ラベルスプライト\n      let labelSprite: THREE.Sprite | undefined;\n      let labelTexture: THREE.CanvasTexture | undefined;\n      if (body.id !== 'sun') {\n        const labelObj = createLabelSprite(body.name);\n        labelSprite = labelObj.sprite;\n        labelTexture = labelObj.texture;\n        labelSprite.position.set(0, body.radius + 2.5, 0);\n        mesh.add(labelSprite);\n        labelSprite.visible = settingsRef.current.showLabels;\n      }\n\n      planetObjects.push({\n        id: body.id,\n        body,\n        mesh,\n        group: orbitGroup,\n        satelliteGroup,\n        orbitLine,\n        labelSprite,\n        labelTexture,\n        currentAngle: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,\n        moons: moonMeshList.length > 0 ? moonMeshList : undefined,\n      });\n    });\n\n    // 内部ステート初期化\n    refs.current = {\n      scene,\n      camera,\n      renderer,\n      planetObjects,\n      isInteracting: false,\n      prevPointer: { x: 0, y: 0 },\n      spherical: initialSpherical,\n      targetFocus: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0),\n      currentFocus: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 0),\n      cameraDistance: 110,\n      targetCameraDistance: 110,\n      reqId: null,\n    };\n\n    // 7. マウス・タッチ操作（OrbitControls相当の軽量実装）\n    const onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (e.button !== 0 && e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      refs.current.isInteracting = true;\n      refs.current.prevPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n    };\n\n    const onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {\n      if (!refs.current || !refs.current.isInteracting) return;\n      const dx = e.clientX - refs.current.prevPointer.x;\n      const dy = e.clientY - refs.current.prevPointer.y;\n      refs.current.prevPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };\n\n      const rotateSpeed = 0.005;\n      refs.current.spherical.theta -= dx * rotateSpeed;\n      refs.current.spherical.phi -= dy * rotateSpeed;\n\n      // 仰角制限 (真上・真下でのフリップ防止)\n      refs.current.spherical.phi = Math.max(0.08, Math.min(Math.PI - 0.08, refs.current.spherical.phi));\n    };\n\n    const onPointerUp = () => {\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      refs.current.isInteracting = false;\n    };\n\n    const onWheel = (e: WheelEvent) => {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      if (!refs.current) return;\n      const zoomFactor = 1 + e.deltaY * 0.0012;\n      refs.current.targetCameraDistance = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(\n        refs.current.targetCameraDistance * zoomFactor,\n        6,\n        350\n      );\n    };\n\n    // レイキャストクリック判定\n    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();\n    const mouseVector = new THREE.Vector2();\n\n    const onClick = (e: MouseEvent) => {\n      if (!refs.current || !container) return;\n      const rect = container.getBoundingClientRect();\n      mouseVector.x = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;\n      mouseVector.y = -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;\n\n      raycaster.setFromCamera(mouseVector, refs.current.camera);\n      const meshesToTest = refs.current.planetObjects.map((po) => po.mesh);\n      const intersects = raycaster.intersectObjects(meshesToTest, false);\n\n      if (intersects.length > 0) {\n        const hitMesh = intersects[0].object as THREE.Mesh;\n        const matched = refs.current.planetObjects.find((po) => po.mesh === hitMesh);\n        if (matched) {\n          onSelectPlanet?.(matched.id);\n        }\n      }\n    };\n\n    const domElement = renderer.domElement;\n    domElement.addEventListener('pointerdown', onPointerDown);\n    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onPointerMove);\n    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onPointerUp);\n    domElement.addEventListener('wheel', onWheel, { passive: false });\n    domElement.addEventListener('click', onClick);\n\n    // リサイズハンドラ\n    const handleResize = () => {\n      if (!container || !refs.current) return;\n      const w = container.clientWidth;\n      const h = container.clientHeight;\n      refs.current.camera.aspect = w / h;\n      refs.current.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();\n      refs.current.renderer.setSize(w, h);\n    };\n    window.addEventListener('resize', handleResize);\n\n    // 8. メインレンダリングループ\n    let lastTime = performance.now();\n    const animate = (time: number) => {\n      const delta = Math.min((time - lastTime) / 1000, 0.1);\n      lastTime = time;\n\n      const state = refs.current;\n      if (!state) return;\n\n      const currentSettings = settingsRef.current;\n      const currentSelectedId = currentSettings.speedMultiplier * delta * 2.5;\n          po.currentAngle += moveDelta;\n          po.mesh.position.x = Math.cos(po.currentAngle) * po.body.distance;\n          po.mesh.position.z = Math.sin(po.currentAngle) * po.body.distance;\n          po.mesh.rotation.y += po.body.rotationSpeed * delta * 60;\n\n          // 衛星の公転\n          if (po.moons && po.moons.length > 0) {\n mo.mesh.position.x = Math.cos(mo.currentAngle) * mo.data.distance;\n              mo.mesh.position.z = Math.sin(mo.currentAngle) * mo.data.distance;\n            });\n          }\n        }\n\n (currentSelectedId === po.id) {\n          po.mesh.getWorldPosition(state.targetFocus);\n        }\n      });\n\n      // 惑星追従時: ユーザーのドラッグ回転と目標位置追従が競合して揺れないよう、\n      // 選択中は直接追従、未選択時はスムーズ補間(Lerp)で原点へ復帰\n      if (currentSelectedId) {\n        state.currentFocus.copy(state.targetFocus);\n      } else {\n        state.currentFocus.lerp(state.targetFocus, 0.06);\n (独立した球面座標オフセットをターゲット位置に加算)\n      const camOffset = new THREE.Vector3().setFromSpherical(state.spherical);\n      state.camera.position.copy(state.currentFocus).add(camOffset);\n requestAnimationFrame(animate);\n\n    // クリーンアップ\n    return () => {\n      if (refs.current?.reqId) {\n        cancelAnimationFrame(refs.current.reqId);\n      }\n
